# Appsel Backend Phase Ledger

This ledger records repository-backed phases only. A phase may be used as a parent authority only when it has a real report and verified repository provenance. `MASTER_ROADMAP.md` planned entries are not parent authorities.

**Phase types**: `EVIDENCE` · `DECISION` · `ARCHITECTURE_DESIGN` · `DESIGN_VERIFICATION` · `IMPLEMENTATION` · `VERIFICATION_CLOSURE` · `CHECKPOINT` · `GOVERNANCE`

**Execution Status is separate from Final Classification.** A phase can have Execution Status `DONE` while its Final Classification records a blocker (e.g. `FREQ6D3_BLOCKED_ON_ARCHITECTURE_DECISION`) — the assessment/design/implementation work itself completed; what it *found* may still be a blocker.

**Provenance**: `VERIFIED` (report file exists in this repo at the listed path, and the listed commit SHA is reachable from the HEAD this ledger was bootstrapped from) or `PROVENANCE_UNVERIFIED` (existence or attribution could not be conclusively established in this pass — never guessed).

All report links are repository-relative. Ledger bootstrapped at governance phase `APPSEL-BACKEND.GOV.0`, from HEAD `6734b00db43de67f1d21e1121bf2ab88e7006a4a` (branch `main`).

---

## Scope note (read before using this ledger)

A repository-wide inventory found **239** tracked root-level phase/checkpoint/governance markdown files. This bootstrap fully ledgers the **currently active 10K program** (`PHASE_10K_GEN_*` and `PHASE_10K_FREQ_*`, 35 reports) plus this governance phase itself, because those are the phases realistically usable as parent authority for near-term work. The remaining ~203 pre-10K-program historical reports (`PHASE0`–`PHASE4M`, `PHASE4A`–`PHASE4L` series, and two root governance notes) predate this ledger discipline and are recorded below as a single bulk `PROVENANCE_UNVERIFIED` range pending a dedicated future backfill governance phase — not because they are doubted, but because this phase did not individually re-verify each one's final classification and exact commit against its own text, and this ledger's own rule forbids inventing that. **Do not use any pre-10K-program report as a parent authority until it has its own ledger row with `VERIFIED` provenance.**

Explicitly prohibited numeric interpolation for every training-load field except pace/finish-time equivalence (the one place a physiologically-established formula already exists), rejected a new discrete "15K-type" structural family for lack of evidence, rejected the `SIXTEEN_K_MASTER`-per-distance anti-pattern as directly contradicting the phase's own reusability goal, and confirmed `TargetDistanceKm` should remain a runtime projection input onto the existing HALF_MARATHON combination files rather than new catalog identity, avoiding catalog explosion entirely. Selected Intermediate 4D as the single next pilot cell (HM's structurally simplest frequency, richest cross-family precedent, isolates the distance-projection question cleanly from the dual-KEY questions HM-X1 already solved separately). Complete retry now returns an idempotent 200 for an exact-value replay and a typed 409 (TrainingDayCompletionConflictException) for a conflicting-value retry, with zero new WorkoutLog/PlanEvent rows either way; NotToday retry is idempotent at both the Create layer (returns the existing Pending decision instead of creating a duplicate) and the Confirm layer (returns the existing Confirmed result instead of re-running adaptation or duplicating the WorkoutMissed PlanEvent). Added a third typed exception (TrainingDayMutationConcurrencyConflictException, 409) for the residual DbUpdateConcurrencyException case.
